{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CezAUxDGBJA5kU2oQPPdw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LFk9oi3hCtYC4SAMtMJxNv","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/black-scholes_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_hybrid_r4/black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp","content":"// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA Black-Scholes option pricing benchmark.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI   distributes the global option range across ranks (one rank per GPU).\n//   * CUDA  prices the bulk of each rank's range on its GPU; option inputs are\n//           generated on the fly on the device, so only results cross PCIe.\n//   * OpenMP drives the GPU pipeline (multi-stream, pinned memory) from one\n//           thread while the remaining threads price options on the CPU cores,\n//           pulling from the same atomic work queue so CPU and GPU self-balance.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <atomic>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                           \\\n    do {                                                                                           \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                            \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__,          \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                                      \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                            \\\n        }                                                                                           \\\n    } while (0)\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nstatic constexpr size_t NUM_TEST_OPTIONS = 7;\n\n// Device-side copy of the test option table\n__constant__ OptionInput c_testOptions[NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n\n// Generate option i of the global sequence (identical to the original\n// generateOptions(), but computed on demand instead of materialized).\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const size_t i) noexcept {\n#ifdef __CUDA_ARCH__\n    OptionInput o = c_testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#else\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    OptionInput o = testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#endif\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    o.spot *= factor;\n    o.strike *= factor;\n    return o;\n}\n\n// Price [globalStart, globalStart + count) into out[0, count)\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t globalStart,\n                                   const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;\n         i += stride) {\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(globalStart + i));\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Work queue granularity. The GPU grabs GPU_GRAB chunks at a time, CPU threads\n// take one chunk at a time, so both sides stay busy until the range is drained.\nstatic constexpr size_t CHUNK_SIZE = 1u << 18;\nstatic constexpr size_t GPU_GRAB = 16;\nstatic constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 3;\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // ---- Bind this rank to a GPU (round-robin within the node) ----\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on rank %d\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr)); // establish the context outside the timed region\n\n    {\n        constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_testOptions, testOptions.data(),\n                                      sizeof(OptionInput) * NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    }\n\n    // ---- Distribute the global option range ----\n    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t myStart = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), prices the bulk of each rank's range on its GPU; option inputs are\n//           generated on the fly on the device, so only results cross PCIe.\n//   * OpenMP drives the GPU pipeline (multi-stream,  double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * option range across ranks (one rank per GPU).\n//   * CUDA  prices the bulk of each rank's range on its GPU; option inputs are\n//           generated on the fly on the device, so only results cross PCIe.\n//   * OpenMP drives the GPU pipeline (multi-stream, pinned memory) from one\n//           thread while the remaining threads price options on the CPU cores,\n//           pulling from the same atomic work queue so CPU and GPU self-balance.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <atomic>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                           \\\n    do {                                                                                           \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                            \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__,          \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                                      \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                            \\\n        }                                                                                           \\\n    } while (0)\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nstatic constexpr size_t NUM_TEST_OPTIONS = 7;\n\n// Device-side copy of the test option table\n__constant__ OptionInput c_testOptions[NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n\n// Generate option i of the global sequence (identical to the original\n// generateOptions(), but computed on demand instead of materialized).\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const size_t i) noexcept {\n#ifdef __CUDA_ARCH__\n    OptionInput o = c_testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#else\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    OptionInput o = testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#endif\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    o.spot *= factor;\n    o.strike *= factor;\n    return o;\n}\n\n// Price [globalStart, globalStart + count) into out[0, count)\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t globalStart,\n                                   const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;\n         i += stride) {\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(globalStart + i));\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases GPU; option inputs are\n//           generated on the fly on the device, so only results cross PCIe.\n//   * OpenMP drives the GPU pipeline (multi-stream, pinned memory) from one\n//           thread Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Work queue granularity. The GPU grabs GPU_GRAB chunks at a time, CPU threads\n// take one chunk at a time, so both sides stay busy until the range is drained.\nstatic constexpr size_t CHUNK_SIZE = 1u << 18;\nstatic constexpr size_t GPU_GRAB = 16;\nstatic constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 3;\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // ---- Bind this rank to a GPU (round-robin within the node) ----\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0, localSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &localSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    // ---- Choose the OpenMP team size ----\n    // Honour an explicit OMP_NUM_THREADS; otherwise take the cores this process\n    // may run on and, if the ranks of this node are unbound (all share the same\n    // full-machine affinity mask), split them evenly between those ranks.\n    int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        int usable = numThreads;\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            usable = CPU_COUNT(&mask);\n        }\n        const long onlineCores = sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n        if (onlineCores > 0 && usable >= static_cast<int>(onlineCores) && localSize > 1) {\n            usable /= localSize;\n        }\n        numThreads = std::max(1, usable);\n    }\n    omp_set_num_threads(numThreads);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on rank %d\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr)); // establish the context outside the timed region\n\n    {\n        constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_testOptions, testOptions.data(),\n                                      sizeof(OptionInput) * NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    }\n\n    // ---- Distribute the global option range ----\n    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t myStart = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n    const size_t myCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n\n    // Pinned host buffer so the GPU can DMA results straight into it\n    double* localResults = nullptr;\n    if (myCount > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&localResults, myCount * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    const size_t numChunks = (myCount + CHUNK_SIZE - 1) / CHUNK_SIZE;\n    const size_t streamCapacity = GPU_GRAB * CHUNK_SIZE;\n\n    cudaStream_t streams[NUM_STREAMS];\n    double* deviceBuffers[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr};\n    const size_t deviceChunkElems = std::min(streamCapacity, myCount > 0 ? myCount : size_t{1});\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceBuffers[s], deviceChunkElems * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    int smCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, localRank % deviceCount));\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n    const int maxBlocks = smCount * 32;\n\n    // Warm up the kernel so JIT/module load does not pollute the measurement\n    blackScholesKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, streams[0]>>>(deviceBuffers[0], 0, 1);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[0]));\n\n    std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Price one queued chunk on the CPU; returns false when the queue is empty.\n    const auto priceOneCpuChunk = [&]() -> bool {\n        const size_t chunk = nextChunk.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n        if (chunk >= numChunks) return false;\n        const size_t begin = chunk * CHUNK_SIZE;\n        const size_t end = std::min(myCount, begin + CHUNK_SIZE);\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            localResults[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(myStart + i));\n        }\n        return true;\n    };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            // ---- GPU pipeline driver ----\n            // While a stream is in flight this thread prices CPU chunks instead of\n            // blocking, so the core it owns is never idle.\n            bool busy[NUM_STREAMS] = {false};\n            int s = 0;\n            while (true) {\n                while (busy[s] && cudaStreamQuery(streams[s]) != cudaSuccess) {\n                    priceOneCpuChunk();\n                }\n                busy[s] = false;\n\n                const size_t chunk = nextChunk.fetch_add(GPU_GRAB, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                if (chunk >= numChunks) break;\n\n                const size_t begin = chunk * CHUNK_SIZE;\n                const size_t end = std::min(myCount, (chunk + GPU_GRAB) * CHUNK_SIZE);\n                const size_t count = end - begin;\n\n                const int blocks = static_cast<int>(\n                    std::min<size_t>(maxBlocks, (count + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE));\n                blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, streams[s]>>>(deviceBuffers[s],\n                                                                         myStart + begin, count);\n                cudaMemcpyAsync(localResults + begin, deviceBuffers[s], count * sizeof(double),\n                                cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams[s]);\n                busy[s] = true;\n                s = (s + 1) % NUM_STREAMS;\n            }\n            for (int i = 0; i < NUM_STREAMS; ++i) {\n                while (busy[i] && cudaStreamQuery(streams[i]) != cudaSuccess) {\n                    priceOneCpuChunk();\n                }\n                if (busy[i]) cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[i]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // ---- CPU workers (thread 0 joins in once the GPU queue is drained) ----\n        while (priceOneCpuChunk()) {\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    long long localMicros =\n        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start).count();\n    long long micros = 0;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMicros, &micros, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", micros / 1000.0);\n        printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (micros / 1e6));\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> all;\n        std::vector<int> counts(nranks), displs(nranks);\n        for (int p = 0; p < nranks; ++p) {\n            const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n            const size_t d = static_cast<size_t>(p) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), rem);\n            counts[p] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            displs[p] = static_cast<int>(d);\n        }\n        if (rank == 0) all.resize(numOptions);\n        MPI_Gatherv(localResults, static_cast<int>(myCount), MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), counts.data(),\n                    displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            print_results(all, \"OptionPrices\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        // Collect the first (up to) 10 computed prices on rank 0; each index is\n        // owned by exactly one rank, so a sum-reduction picks up the right value.\n        const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);\n        std::array<double, 10> mine{}, gathered{};\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n            if (i >= myStart && i < myStart + myCount) mine[i] = localResults[i - myStart];\n        }\n        MPI_Reduce(mine.data(), gathered.data(), 10, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        int status = 0;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n            std::vector<OptionInput> headOptions(numChecks);\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) headOptions[i] = makeOption(i);\n            std::vector<double> headResults(gathered.begin(), gathered.begin() + numChecks);\n            const bool valid = validateResults(headOptions, headResults);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n            cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]);\n            cudaFree(deviceBuffers[s]);\n        }\n        if (localResults) cudaFreeHost(localResults);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]);\n        cudaFree(deviceBuffers[s]);\n    }\n    if (localResults) cudaFreeHost(localResults);\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":18,"oldLines":6,"newStart":18,"newLines":9,"lines":[" #include <cstring>"," #include <vector>"," ","+#include <sched.h>","+#include <unistd.h>","+"," #include <cuda_runtime.h>"," #include <mpi.h>"," #include <omp.h>"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

per device batch\nstatic constexpr size_t CPU_GRAB = 4;      // chunks a CPU worker takes at once","originalFile":"// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA Black-Scholes option pricing benchmark.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI   distributes the global option range across ranks (one rank per GPU).\n//   * CUDA  prices the bulk of each rank's range on its GPU; option inputs are\n//           generated on the fly on the device, so only results cross PCIe.\n//   * OpenMP drives the GPU pipeline (multi-stream, pinned memory) from one\n//           thread while the remaining threads price options on the CPU cores,\n//           pulling from the same atomic work queue so CPU and GPU self-balance.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <atomic>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <sched.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                       \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                            \\\n        }                                                                                           \\\n    } while (0)\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nstatic constexpr size_t NUM_TEST_OPTIONS = 7;\n\n// Device-side copy of the test option table\n__constant__ OptionInput c_testOptions[NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n\n// Generate option i of the global sequence (identical to the original\n// generateOptions(), but computed on demand instead of materialized).\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const size_t i) noexcept {\n#ifdef __CUDA_ARCH__\n    OptionInput o = c_testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#else\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    OptionInput o = testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#endif\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    o.spot *= factor;\n    o.strike *= factor;\n    return o;\n}\n\n// Price [globalStart, globalStart + count) into out[0, count)\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t globalStart,\n                                   const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;\n         i += stride) {\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(globalStart + i));\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Work queue granularity. The GPU grabs GPU_GRAB chunks at a time, CPU threads\n// take one chunk at a time, so both sides stay busy until the range is drained.\nstatic constexpr size_t CHUNK_SIZE = 1u << 18;\nstatic constexpr size_t GPU_GRAB = 16;\nstatic constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 3;\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // ---- Bind this rank to a GPU (round-robin within the node) ----\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0, localSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &localSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    // ---- Choose the OpenMP team size ----\n    // Honour an explicit OMP_NUM_THREADS; otherwise take the cores this process\n    // may run on and, if the ranks of this node are unbound (all share the same\n    // full-machine affinity mask), split them evenly between those ranks.\n    int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        int usable = numThreads;\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            usable = CPU_COUNT(&mask);\n        }\n        const long onlineCores = sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n        if (onlineCores > 0 && usable >= static_cast<int>(onlineCores) && localSize > 1) {\n            usable /= localSize;\n        }\n        numThreads = std::max(1, usable);\n    }\n    omp_set_num_threads(numThreads);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on rank %d\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr)); s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceBuffers[s], deviceChunkElems * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    int smCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, localRank % deviceCount));\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n    const int maxBlocks = smCount * 32;\n\n    // Warm up the kernel so JIT/module load does not pollute the measurement\n    blackScholesKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, streams[0]>>>(deviceBuffers[0], 0, 1);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[0]));\n\n    std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Price one queued chunk on the CPU; i < NUM_STREAMS; ++i) {\n                while (busy[i] && cudaStreamQuery(streams[i]) != cudaSuccess) {\n                    priceOneCpuChunk();\n                }\n                if (busy[i]) cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[i]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // ---- CPU workers (thread 0 joins in once the GPU queue is drained) ----\n        while (priceOneCpuChunk()) {\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    long long localMicros =\n        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start).count();\n    long long micros = 0;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMicros, &micros, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", micros / 1000.0);\n        printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (micros / 1e6));\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> all;\n        std::vector<int> counts(nranks), displs(nranks);\n        for (int p = 0; p < nranks; ++p) {\n            const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n            const size_t d = static_cast<size_t>(p) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), rem);\n            counts[p] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            displs[p] = static_cast<int>(d);\n        }\n        if (rank == 0) all.resize(numOptions);\n        MPI_Gatherv(localResults, static_cast<int>(myCount), MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), counts.data(),\n                    displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            print_results(all, \"OptionPrices\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        // Collect the first (up to) 10 computed prices on rank 0; each index is\n        // owned by exactly one rank, so a sum-reduction picks up the right value.\n        const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);\n        std::array<double, 10> mine{}, gathered{};\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n            if (i >= myStart && i < myStart + myCount) mine[i] = localResults[i - myStart];\n        }\n        MPI_Reduce(mine.data(), gathered.data(), 10, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        int status = 0;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n            std::vector<OptionInput> headOptions(numChecks);\n            for (size_t i = 0; global option range across ranks (one rank per GPU).\n//   * CUDA  prices the bulk of each rank's range on its GPU; option inputs are\n//           generated on the fly on the device, so only results cross PCIe.\n//   * OpenMP drives the GPU pipeline (multi-stream, pinned memory) from one\n//           thread while the remaining threads price options on the CPU cores,\n//           pulling from the same atomic work queue so CPU and GPU self-balance.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <atomic>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                           \\\n    do {                                                                                           \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                            \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                                                      \\\n        }                                                                                           \\\n    } while (0)\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00,   // ~4M options per device batch\nstatic constexpr size_t CPU_GRAB = 4;      // chunks a CPU worker takes at once\nstatic constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 3;\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // ---- Bind this rank to a GPU (round-robin within the node) ----\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0, localSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &localSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    // ---- Choose the OpenMP team size ----\n    // Honour an explicit OMP_NUM_THREADS; otherwise take the cores this process\n    // may run on and, if the ranks of this node are unbound (all share the same\n    // full-machine affinity mask), split them evenly between those ranks.\n    int numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr) {\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        int usable = numThreads;\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            usable = CPU_COUNT(&mask);\n        }\n        const long onlineCores = sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n        if (onlineCores > 0 && usable >= static_cast<int>(onlineCores) && localSize > 1) {\n            usable /= localSize;\n        }\n        numThreads = std::max(1, usable);\n    }\n    omp_set_num_threads(numThreads);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on rank %d\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr)); // establish the context outside the timed region\n\n    {\n        constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_testOptions, testOptions.data(),\n                                      sizeof(OptionInput) * NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    }\n\n    // ---- Distribute the global option range ----\n    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t myStart = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n    const size_t myCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n\n    // Pinned host buffer so the GPU can DMA results straight into it\n    double* localResults = nullptr;\n    if (myCount > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&localResults, myCount * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    const size_t numChunks = (myCount + CHUNK_SIZE - 1) / CHUNK_SIZE;\n    const size_t streamCapacity = GPU_GRAB * CHUNK_SIZE;\n\n    cudaStream_t streams[NUM_STREAMS];\n    double* deviceBuffers[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr};\n    const size_t deviceChunkElems = std::min(streamCapacity, myCount > 0 ? myCount : size_t{1});\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceBuffers[s], deviceChunkElems * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    int smCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, localRank % deviceCount));\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n    const int maxBlocks = smCount * 32;\n\n    // Warm up the kernel so JIT/module load does not pollute the measurement\n    blackScholesKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, streams[0]>>>(deviceBuffers[0], 0, 1);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[0]));\n\n    std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Price one queued chunk on the CPU; returns false when the queue is empty.\n    const auto priceOneCpuChunk = [&]() -> bool {\n        const size_t chunk = nextChunk.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n        if (chunk >= numChunks) return false;\n        const size_t begin = chunk * CHUNK_SIZE;\n        const size_t end = std::min(myCount, begin + CHUNK_SIZE);\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            localResults[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(myStart + i));\n        }\n        return true;\n    };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            // ---- GPU pipeline driver ----\n            // While a stream is in flight this thread prices CPU chunks instead of\n            // blocking, so the core it owns is never idle.\n            bool busy[NUM_STREAMS] = {false};\n            int s = 0;\n            while (true) {\n                while (busy[s] && cudaStreamQuery(streams[s]) != cudaSuccess) {\n                    priceOneCpuChunk();\n                }\n                busy[s] = false;\n\n                const size_t chunk = nextChunk.fetch_add(GPU_GRAB, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                if (chunk >= numChunks) break;\n\n                const size_t begin = chunk * CHUNK_SIZE;\n                const size_t end = std::min(myCount, (chunk + GPU_GRAB) * CHUNK_SIZE);\n                const size_t count = end - begin;\n\n                const int blocks = static_cast<int>(\n                    std::min<size_t>(maxBlocks, (count + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE));\n                blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, streams[s]>>>(deviceBuffers[s],\n                                                                         myStart + begin, count);\n                cudaMemcpyAsync(localResults + begin, deviceBuffers[s], count * sizeof(double),\n                                cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams[s]);\n                busy[s] = true;\n                s = (s + 1) % NUM_STREAMS;\n            }\n            for (int i = 0; i < NUM_STREAMS; ++i) {\n                while (busy[i] && cudaStreamQuery(streams[i]) != cudaSuccess) {\n                    priceOneCpuChunk();\n                }\n                if (busy[i]) cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[i]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // ---- CPU workers (thread 0 joins in once the GPU queue is drained) ----\n        while (priceOneCpuChunk()) {\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    long long localMicros =\n        std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start).count();\n    long long micros = 0;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMicros, &micros, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", micros / 1000.0);\n        printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (micros / 1e6));\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        std::vector<double> all;\n        std::vector<int> counts(nranks), displs(nranks);\n        for (int p = 0; p < nranks; ++p) {\n            const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n            const size_t d = static_cast<size_t>(p) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), rem);\n            counts[p] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            displs[p] = static_cast<int>(d);\n        }\n        if (rank == 0) all.resize(numOptions);\n        MPI_Gatherv(localResults, static_cast<int>(myCount), MPI_DOUBLE, all.data(), counts.data(),\n                    displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            print_results(all, \"OptionPrices\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        // Collect the first (up to) 10 computed prices on rank 0; each index is\n        // owned by exactly one rank, so a sum-reduction picks up the right value.\n        const size_t numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), numOptions);\n        std::array<double, 10> mine{}, gathered{};\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n            if (i >= myStart && i < myStart + myCount) mine[i] = localResults[i - myStart];\n        }\n        MPI_Reduce(mine.data(), gathered.data(), 10, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        int status = 0;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n            std::vector<OptionInput> headOptions(numChecks);\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) headOptions[i] = makeOption(i);\n            std::vector<double> headResults(gathered.begin(), gathered.begin() + numChecks);\n            const bool valid = validateResults(headOptions, headResults);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n            cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]);\n            cudaFree(deviceBuffers[s]);\n        }\n        if (localResults) cudaFreeHost(localResults);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams[s]);\n        cudaFree(deviceBuffers[s]);\n    }\n    if (localResults) cudaFreeHost(localResults);\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":314,"oldLines":12,"newStart":314,"newLines":12,"lines":["     MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);","     auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();"," ","-    // Price one queued chunk on the CPU;      // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n//   {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, the global option range across ranks (one rank per GPU).\n//   * CUDA  prices the bulk of each rank's range on its GPU; option inputs are\n//           generated on the fly on the device, so only results cross PCIe.\n//   * OpenMP drives the GPU pipeline (multi-stream, pinned memory) from one\n//           thread while the remaining threads price options on the CPU cores,\n//           pulling from the same atomic work queue so CPU and GPU self-balance.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <atomic>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                           \\\n    do {                                                                                           \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                            \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                  \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                                                      \\\n        }                                                                                           \\\n    } while (0)\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\nstatic constexpr size_t NUM_TEST_OPTIONS = 7;\n\n// Device-side copy of the test option table\n__constant__ OptionInput c_testOptions[NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n\n// Generate option i of the global sequence (identical to the original\n// generateOptions(), but computed on demand instead of materialized).\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const size_t i) noexcept {\n#ifdef __CUDA_ARCH__\n    OptionInput o = c_testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#else\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    OptionInput o = testOptions[i % NUM_TEST_OPTIONS];\n#endif\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    o.spot *= factor;\n    o.strike *= factor;\n    return o;\n}\n\n// Price [globalStart, globalStart + count) into out[0, count)\n__global__ void blackScholesKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t globalStart,\n                                   const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;\n         i += stride) {\n        out[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(globalStart + i));\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Work queue granularity. The GPU grabs GPU_GRAB chunks at a time, CPU threads\n// take one chunk at a time, so both sides stay busy until the range is drained.\n#ifndef TCHUNK\n#define TCHUNK 12\n#endif\n#ifndef TGRAB\n#define TGRAB 1024\n#endif\n#ifndef TSTREAMS\n#define TSTREAMS 3\n#endif\n#ifndef TBLK\n#define TBLK 32\n#endif\nstatic constexpr size_t CHUNK_SIZE = 1u << TCHUNK;\nstatic constexpr size_t GPU_GRAB = TGRAB;   // ~4M options per device batch\nstatic constexpr size_t CPU_GRAB = 4;      // chunks a CPU worker takes at once\nstatic constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = TSTREAMS;\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // ---- Bind this rank to a GPU (round-robin within the node) ----\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0, localSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &localSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    // ---- Choose the OpenMP team size ----\n    // Honour an explicit OMP_NUM_THREADS; otherwise take the cores this process\n    // may run on and, if the ranks of this node are unbound (all share the same\n    // full-machine affinity mask), split them evenly between those ranks.\n    std::vector<int> myCpus;      // CPUs this rank pins its OpenMP threads to\n    {\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        std::vector<int> cpus;\n        if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) == 0) {\n            for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n                if (CPU_ISSET(c, &mask)) cpus.push_back(c);\n            }\n        }\n        const long onlineCores = sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);\n        const bool unbound =\n            onlineCores > 0 && static_cast<long>(cpus.size()) >= onlineCores && localSize > 1;\n        if (unbound) {\n            // All node-local ranks see the whole machine: carve out a private slice\n            // so the ranks do not fight over the same cores.\n            const size_t per = cpus.size() / static_cast<size_t>(localSize);\n            const size_t first = per * static_cast<size_t>(localRank);\n            myCpus.assign(cpus.begin() + first, cpus.begin() + first + per);\n        } else {\n            myCpus = cpus;\n        }\n    }\n\n    int numThreads = static_cast<int>(myCpus.size());\n    if (const char* envThreads = getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\")) {\n        numThreads = atoi(envThreads);\n    }\n    if (numThreads < 1) numThreads = 1;\n    omp_set_num_threads(numThreads);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on rank %d\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr)); // establish the context outside the timed region\n\n    {\n        constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_testOptions, testOptions.data(),\n                                      sizeof(OptionInput) * NUM_TEST_OPTIONS));\n    }\n\n    // ---- Distribute the global option range ----\n    const size_t base = numOptions / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = numOptions % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t myStart = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n    const size_t myCount = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n\n    // Pinned host buffer so the GPU can DMA results straight into it\n    double* localResults = nullptr;\n    if (myCount > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&localResults, myCount * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    const size_t numChunks = (myCount + CHUNK_SIZE - 1) / CHUNK_SIZE;\n    const size_t streamCapacity = GPU_GRAB * CHUNK_SIZE;\n\n    cudaStream_t streams[NUM_STREAMS];\n    double* deviceBuffers[NUM_STREAMS] = {nullptr};\n    const size_t deviceChunkElems = std::min(streamCapacity, myCount > 0 ? myCount : size_t{1});\n    for (int s = 0; s < NUM_STREAMS; ++s) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams[s]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceBuffers[s], deviceChunkElems * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    int smCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&smCount, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, localRank % deviceCount));\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n    const int maxBlocks = smCount * TBLK;\n\n    // Warm up the kernel so JIT/module load does not pollute the measurement\n    blackScholesKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, streams[0]>>>(deviceBuffers[0], 0, 1);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams[0]));\n\n    std::atomic<size_t> nextChunk{0};\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Price the next `grab` queued chunks on the CPU; false when the queue is empty.\n    const auto priceCpuChunks = [&](const size_t grab) -> bool {\n        const size_t chunk = nextChunk.fetch_add(grab, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n        if (chunk >= numChunks) return false;\n        const size_t begin = chunk * CHUNK_SIZE;\n        const size_t end = std::min(myCount, (chunk + grab) * CHUNK_SIZE);\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            localResults[i] = blackScholes(makeOption(myStart + i));\n        }\n        return true;\n    };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            // ---- GPU pipeline driver ----\n            // While a stream is in flight this thread prices CPU chunks instead of\n            // blocking, so the core it owns is never idle.\n            bool busy[NUM_STREAMS] = {false};\n            int s = 0;\n            while (true) {\n                while (busy[s] && cudaStreamQuery(streams[s]) != cudaSuccess) {\n                    priceCpuChunks(1);\n                }\n                busy[s] = false;\n\n                const size_t chunk = nextChunk.fetch_add(GPU_GRAB, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                if (chunk >= numChunks) break;\n\n                const size_t begin = chunk * CHUNK_SIZE;\n                const size_t end = std::min(myCount, (chunk + GPU_GRAB) * CHUNK_SIZE);\n                const size_t count = end - begin;\n\n                const int blocks = static_cast<int>(\n                    std::min<size_t>(maxBlocks, (count + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE));\n                blackScholesKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, streams[s]>>>(deviceBuffers[s],\n                                                                         myStart + begin, count);\n                cudaMemcpyAsync(localResults + begin, deviceBuffers[s], count * sizeof(double),\n                                cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams[s]);\n                busy[s] = true;\n                s = (s + 1) % NUM_STREAMS;\n            }\n            for (int i = 0;
